<div id="get-accountsaccount_idcurated_categories">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/curated_categories[](#get-accounts-account-id-curated-categories "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

检索给定 `country_codes` 对应的可用精选分类列表。

每个 `curated_category` 仅在特定国家/地区可用，这些国家/地区在响应中的 `country_codes` 字段中指定。

更多详细信息请参阅 [Video Views Pre-roll Objective 指南](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#video-views-preroll-objective)。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/curated_categories`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 被使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| country&#95;codes  <br />*required* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的双字母 ISO 国家/地区代码列表，将响应限定为所需的国家/地区。最多可提供 200 个代码。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `US` |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息请参阅 [分页](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/curated_categories?country_codes=US`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idcurated_categoriescurated_category_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/curated_categories/:curated_category_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-curated-categories-curated-category-id "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

检索特定 `curated_category_id` 的详细信息

每个 `curated_category` 仅在特定国家/地区可用，这些国家/地区由响应中的 `country_codes` 字段指定。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/curated_categories/:curated_category_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                          | Description                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| :-------------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*              | 要使用的账户标识符。该字段出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Ads API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与经过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| curated&#95;category&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的 Curated Category 的标识符。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `9ddrgesiap6o`                                                                                                    |

**Example Request[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/curated_categories/9ddrgesiap6o`

**Example Response[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

<div id="features">
  ### 功能特性
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idfeatures">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/features[](#get-accounts-account-id-features "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

检索此广告账户可访问的已授予功能集合。功能通过具有描述性的功能键标识，并且仅当它们作为测试版或其他受限发布的一部分引入且在 Ads API 中可用时，才会在此端点中返回。不符合此条件的功能不会通过此端点暴露。

**注意**：此端点旨在通过提升对客户端测试版访问权限的可见性来帮助 Ads API 生态系统开发。API 开发者无法代表广告主请求访问功能。这类请求只能由广告主向其 X 客户经理提出。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/features`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                               | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| :--------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*   | 被使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求中的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`               |
| feature&#95;keys  <br />*optional* | 一个可选参数，用于查询特定的功能键。请求可以包含多个以逗号分隔的键。<br /><br />**注意**：只有此账户可访问的功能才会包含在响应中。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `REACH_AND_FREQUENCY_ANALYTICS`, `REACH_FREQUENCY_CAP`, `WEBSITE_CLICKS_CPM_BILLING` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/features`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": [
        "CITY_TARGETING",
        "CONVERSATION_CARD",
        "PROMOTED_MEDIA_POLLS",
        "REACH_AND_FREQUENCY_ANALYTICS",
        "REACH_FREQUENCY_CAP",
        "UNIVERSAL_LOOKALIKE"
      ]
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idfeatures">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/features[](#post-accounts-account-id-features "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

**仅限沙盒环境**

向沙盒账户添加功能。

可通过 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/features](#get-accounts-account-id-features) 端点获取最新的账户功能列表。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/features`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| 名称                           | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| :--------------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必填*   | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所需的必填参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `gq180y`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| feature&#95;keys  <br />*必填* | 要添加到账户的账户功能的逗号分隔列表。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `AGE_TARGETING`, `ALLOW_SKIPPABLE_VIDEOS_FOR_PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`, `AWARENESS_OBJECTIVE`, `BRAND_TPN`, `CHARGE_FOR_GOOD_CLICK`, `CONVERSATION_CARD`, `CONVERSATION_CARD_FOUR_OPTIONS`, `CONVERSATION_CARD_UNLOCK`, `CPI_CHARGING`, `DIRECT_MESSAGE_CARD`, `DR_TAP`, `ENGAGER_RETARGETING`, `EVENT_TARGETING`, `INSTALLED_APP_CATEGORY_TARGETING`, `MOBILE_CONVERSION_TRANSACTION_VALUE`, `OPTIMIZED_ACTION_BIDDING`, `REACH_AND_FREQUENCY_ANALYTICS`, `REACH_FREQUENCY_CAP`, `VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING`, `VIDEO_VIEWS_MIDROLL_OBJECTIVE`, `PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`, `VIDEO_APP_DOWNLOAD_CARD` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`POST https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/gq180y/features?feature_keys=VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING`

**响应示例[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "gq180y",
          "feature_keys": [
            "VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING"
          ]
        }
      },
      "data": [
        "ALLOW_SKIPPABLE_VIDEOS_FOR_PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE",
        "AWARENESS_OBJECTIVE",
        "CPI_CHARGING",
        "EVENT_TARGETING",
        "INSTALLED_APP_CATEGORY_TARGETING",
        "MOBILE_CONVERSION_TRANSACTION_VALUE",
        "OPTIMIZED_ACTION_BIDDING",
        "VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING",
        "VIDEO_APP_DOWNLOAD_CARD"
      ]
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idfeatures">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/features[](#delete-accounts-account-id-features "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

**仅限 SANDBOX**

从沙盒账号中移除某个功能。

可以通过 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/features](#get-accounts-account-id-features) 端点获取最新的账号功能列表。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/features`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                               | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :--------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*   | 目标账号的标识符。它出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 以外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必填参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `gq180y`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| feature&#95;keys  <br />*required* | 以逗号分隔的账号功能列表，用于从账号中移除这些功能。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `AGE_TARGETING`, `ALLOW_SKIPPABLE_VIDEOS_FOR_PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`, `AWARENESS_OBJECTIVE`, `BRAND_TPN`, `CHARGE_FOR_GOOD_CLICK`, `CONVERSATION_CARD`, `CONVERSATION_CARD_FOUR_OPTIONS`, `CONVERSATION_CARD_UNLOCK`, `CPI_CHARGING`, `DIRECT_MESSAGE_CARD`, `DR_TAP`, `ENGAGER_RETARGETING`, `EVENT_TARGETING`, `INSTALLED_APP_CATEGORY_TARGETING`, `MOBILE_CONVERSION_TRANSACTION_VALUE`, `OPTIMIZED_ACTION_BIDDING`, `REACH_AND_FREQUENCY_ANALYTICS`, `REACH_FREQUENCY_CAP`, `VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING`, `VIDEO_VIEWS_MIDROLL_OBJECTIVE`, `PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`, `VIDEO_APP_DOWNLOAD_CARD` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/gq180y/features?feature_keys=PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "gq180y",
          "feature_keys": [
            "PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE"
          ]
        }
      },
      "data": [
        "CPI_CHARGING",
        "EVENT_TARGETING",
        "INSTALLED_APP_CATEGORY_TARGETING",
        "MOBILE_CONVERSION_TRANSACTION_VALUE",
        "OPTIMIZED_ACTION_BIDDING",
        "VIDEO_APP_DOWNLOAD_CARD"
      ]
    }
```

### 资金来源

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idfunding_instruments">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments[](#get-accounts-account-id-funding-instruments "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部资金来源的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                             | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| :----------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                 | 所使用的账户的标识符。该值出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                       |
| count  <br />*optional*                          | 指定每个独立请求要检索的记录数。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                         |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                         | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                                 |
| funding&#95;instrument&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，仅返回所需的资金来源。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `lygyi`                                                                                                                                       |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*                    | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息参见 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                             |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*               | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                           |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional*       | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**Note**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**Note**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/funding_instruments`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "start_time": "2016-07-22T04:24:04Z",
          "description": "Visa ending in 0650",
          "credit_limit_local_micro": 200000000,
          "end_time": null,
          "id": "lygyi",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
          "reasons_not_able_to_fund": [],
          "io_header": null,
          "currency": "USD",
          "funded_amount_local_micro": 645940000,
          "created_at": "2016-07-22T04:24:04Z",
          "type": "CREDIT_CARD",
          "able_to_fund": true,
          "updated_at": "2017-04-05T00:25:13Z",
          "credit_remaining_local_micro": null,
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idfunding_instrumentsfunding_instrument_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments/:funding_instrument_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-funding-instruments-funding-instrument-id "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的特定资金工具。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments/:id`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                            | Description                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| :---------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                | 所使用的账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| funding&#95;instrument&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的资金工具的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `lygyi`                                                                                                                        |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*              | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                  |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/funding_instruments/lygyi`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {

        "description": "Visa ending in 0650",
        "credit_limit_local_micro": 200000000,
        "end_time": null,
        "id": "lygyi",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
        "reasons_not_able_to_fund": [],
        "io_header": null,
        "currency": "USD",
        "funded_amount_local_micro": 645940000,
        "created_at": "2016-07-22T04:24:04Z",
        "type": "CREDIT_CARD",
        "able_to_fund": true,
        "updated_at": "2017-04-05T00:25:13Z",
        "credit_remaining_local_micro": null,
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idfunding_instruments">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments[](#post-accounts-account-id-funding-instruments "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

**仅限 SANDBOX**

在 sandbox 环境中创建资金工具（funding instrument）。

在 sandbox 中使用资金工具不会有产生费用的风险。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                                    | Description                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| :------------------------------------------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                        | 相关账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `gq1844` |
| currency  <br />*required*                              | 货币，以 [ISO-4217](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_4217) 格式表示。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `USD`                                                                             |
| start&#95;time  <br />*required*                        | 资金工具开始生效并可使用的日期，以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `2017-05-19T07:00:00Z`                                               |
| type  <br />*required*                                  | 要创建的资金工具类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `AGENCY_CREDIT_LINE`, `CREDIT_CARD`, `CREDIT_LINE`, `INSERTION_ORDER`, `PARTNER_MANAGED`                                      |
| end&#95;time  <br />*sometimes required*                | 资金工具失效的日期，以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `2017-05-26T07:00:00Z`                                                     |
| credit&#95;limit&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*optional*  | 此资金工具可用的总授信额度。<br /><br />**注意**：仅适用于某些资金工具类型。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `37500000`                                                                                         |
| funded&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*optional* | 分配给此资金工具的预算总金额。<br /><br />**注意**：仅适用于某些资金工具类型。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `37500000`                                                                                        |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/gq1844/funding_instruments?currency=USD&start_time=2017-07-10T00:00:00Z&type=INSERTION_ORDER&end_time=2018-01-10T00:00:00Z&funded_amount_local_micro=140000000000`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "start_time": "2017-07-10T00:00:00Z",
        "description": "(尚未设置支付方式)",
        "credit_limit_local_micro": null,
        "end_time": "2018-01-10T00:00:00Z",
        "id": "hxtet",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "account_id": "gq1844",
        "reasons_not_able_to_fund": [],
        "io_header": null,
        "currency": "USD",
        "funded_amount_local_micro": 140000000000,
        "created_at": "2017-09-09T05:23:28Z",
        "type": "INSERTION_ORDER",
        "able_to_fund": true,
        "updated_at": "2017-09-09T05:23:28Z",
        "credit_remaining_local_micro": null,
        "deleted": false
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "start_time": "2017-07-10T00:00:00Z",
          "end_time": "2018-01-10T00:00:00Z",
          "account_id": "gq1844",
          "currency": "USD",
          "funded_amount_local_micro": 140000000000,
          "type": "INSERTION_ORDER"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idfunding_instrumentsfunding_instrument_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments/:funding_instrument_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-funding-instruments-funding-instrument-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

**仅限 SANDBOX 环境**

在 sandbox 环境中删除一条资金工具（funding instrument）。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments/:funding_instrument_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                            | Description                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :---------------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（不包括 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts)）所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `gq1844` |
| funding&#95;instrument&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在请求中要操作的资金工具。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `hxt82`                                                                                                                             |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/gq1844/funding_instruments/hxt82`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "start_time": "2017-08-30T19:23:47Z",
        "description": "(no payment method has been set up yet)",
        "credit_limit_local_micro": 500000000,
        "end_time": null,
        "id": "hxt82",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "account_id": "gq1844",
        "reasons_not_able_to_fund": [
          "DELETED"
        ],
        "io_header": null,
        "currency": "USD",
        "funded_amount_local_micro": null,
        "created_at": "2017-08-30T19:23:47Z",
        "type": "CREDIT_CARD",
        "able_to_fund": false,
        "updated_at": "2017-09-09T02:08:30Z",
        "credit_remaining_local_micro": null,
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "funding_instrument_id": "hxt82",
          "account_id": "gq1844"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="iab-categories">
  ### IAB 类别
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">

<div id="get-iab_categories">
  #### GET iab_categories[](#get-iab-categories "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

请求用于广告组（`line_items`）的有效 App `categories`。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/iab_categories`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                       | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| :----------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| count  <br />*optional*                    | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                       |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                   | 指定用于获取下一页分类的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `gc-ddf4a`                                                                                                              |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional* | 是否在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**Note**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**Note**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求会有更低的速率限制，目前设为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/iab_categories?count=2`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "id": "IAB1",
          "parent_id": null,
          "name": "Arts & Entertainment"
        },
        {
          "id": "IAB1-1",
          "parent_id": "IAB1",
          "name": "Books & Literature"
        }
      ],
      "next_cursor": "uxa8",
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "count": 2
        }
      }
    }
```

  ### 投放项
</div>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idline_items">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/line_items[](#get-accounts-account-id-line-items "此标题的固定链接")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部 line item 的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "此标题的固定链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items`

**Parameters[](#parameters "此标题的固定链接")**

| Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，一般是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| campaign&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限制为特定广告系列下的 line item。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8gdx6` |
| count  <br />*optional* | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000` |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow` |
| funding&#95;instrument&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限制为特定资金工具下的 line item。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `lygyi` |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限制为所需的 line item。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo` |
| q  <br />*optional* | 一个可选查询，用于按 `name` 限定资源范围。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Min, Max length: `1`, `255` |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional* | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc` |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional* | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |
| with&#95;draft  <br />*optional* | 在请求中包含草稿广告系列的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional* | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**Note**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**Note**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有较低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "此标题的固定链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items?line_item_ids=itttx`

**Example Response[](#example-response "此标题的固定链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
          "line_item_ids": [
            "itttx"
          ]
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "name": "li-18",
          "placements": [
            "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
          ],
          "start_time": "2021-02-16T00:00:00Z",
          "bid_amount_local_micro": 320000,
          "advertiser_domain": null,
          "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
          "primary_web_event_tag": null,
          "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
          "end_time": null,
          "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
          "bid_strategy": "MAX",
          "duration_in_days": null,
          "standard_delivery": null,
          "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
          "id": "itttx",
          "entity_status": "PAUSED",
          "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
          "frequency_cap": null,
          "android_app_store_identifier": null,
          "categories": [],
          "currency": "USD",
          "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "created_at": "2021-02-23T23:37:54Z",
          "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
          "updated_at": "2022-06-01T02:01:18Z",
          "campaign_id": "f4z6x",
          "creative_source": "MANUAL",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idline_itemsline_item_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-line-items-line-item-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的特定 line item。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| :------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在该请求中要操作的 line item 的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                                     |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*     | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                        |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items/itttx`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

        "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
        "end_time": null,
        "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
        "bid_strategy": "MAX",
        "duration_in_days": null,
        "standard_delivery": null,
        "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
        "id": "itttx",
        "entity_status": "PAUSED",
        "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
        "frequency_cap": null,
        "android_app_store_identifier": null,
        "categories": [],
        "currency": "USD",
        "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "created_at": "2021-02-23T23:37:54Z",
        "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
        "updated_at": "2022-06-01T02:01:18Z",
        "campaign_id": "f4z6x",
        "creative_source": "MANUAL",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idline_items">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/line_items[](#post-accounts-account-id-line-items "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

创建一个与当前账户下指定广告系列关联的 line item。

同一广告系列中的所有 line item 必须具有相同的 `product_type` 和 `objective`。

当使用 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 产品类型时，将一条推文关联到该 `line_item`，除了标准的 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 展示位置之外，还会在移动端时间线中新增展示位置。

设置 `android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 任一字段时，系统会自动为该 line item 添加与所推广移动 App 匹配的定向条件；例如，传入 `ios_app_store_identifier` 会为 `iOS` 添加 `PLATFORM` [定向条件](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#targeting-options)。

**注意**：每个广告系列最多可包含 100 个 line item，且在所有广告系列中最多可有 256 个处于激活状态的 line item。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| 名称                                                                | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| :---------------------------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必填*                                        | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）中的必需参数。指定的账户必须与经过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| campaign&#95;id  <br />*必填*                                       | 用于在其下创建该 line item 的 campaign 标识符。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8slvg`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| end&#95;time  <br />*必填*                                          | 以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示的时间点，指示该 line item 将停止投放的时间。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `2017-10-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 推广目标  <br />*必填*                                                  | 此广告项目的投放目标。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`APP_ENGAGEMENTS`、`APP_INSTALLS`、`REACH`、`FOLLOWERS`、`ENGAGEMENTS`、`VIDEO_VIEWS`、`PREROLL_VIEWS`、`WEBSITE_CLICKS`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| placements  <br />*必填*                                            | 此 line item 的投放位置。请指定以逗号分隔的 placement 值列表。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `ALL_ON_TWITTER`, `PUBLISHER_NETWORK`, `TAP_BANNER`, `TAP_FULL`, `TAP_FULL_LANDSCAPE`, `TAP_NATIVE`, `TAP_MRECT`,`TWITTER_PROFILE`, `TWITTER_REPLIES`, `TWITTER_SEARCH`, `TWITTER_TIMELINE`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| product&#95;type  <br />*必需*                                      | 该投放项将包含的推广产品类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`MEDIA`, `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT`, `PROMOTED_TWEETS`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| start&#95;time  <br />*必填*                                        | 以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示的此 line item 开始投放的时间。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `2017-07-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| advertiser&#95;domain  <br />*在某些情况下必填*                           | 此广告主的网站域名，不包含协议部分。<br /><br />**注意**：当 line item 的 placement 设置为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时为必填字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `x.com`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| android&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*有时必需*            | 用于推广应用的 Google Play 商店标识符。<br /><br />**注意**：`APP_INSTALLS` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 广告目标至少需要设置一个应用商店标识符——`android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 之一。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `com.twitter.android`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| bid&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*有时为必填项*                | 要与此 line item 关联的出价金额。将使用与指定 funding instrument 关联的货币。对于 USD，$5.50 表示为 5500000。<br /><br />**注意**：当 `bid_strategy` 设置为 `MAX` 或 `TARGET` 时为必填项。<br /><br />**注意**：仅接受大于零的数值。<br /><br />类型：long<br /><br />示例：`5500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| categories  <br />*有时为必填项*                                        | 该广告主的相关 IAB 类别。请参阅 [GET iab&#95;categories](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#iab-categories)。<br /><br />**注意**：当 line item 的投放位置设置为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时为必填项。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `IAB3-1`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ios&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*在某些情况下必填*            | 用于推广应用的 Apple App Store 标识符中的数字部分。<br /><br />**注意**：当广告目标为 `APP_INSTALLS` 或 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 时，必须至少设置一个应用商店标识符——`android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 之一。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `333903271`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| primary&#95;web&#95;event&#95;tag  <br />*某些情况下必填*                | 主要 Web 事件标签的标识符。可更精确地跟踪与此行项目关联的广告系列中的互动。<br /><br />**注意**：当行项目的目标设置为 `WEBSITE_CONVERSIONS` 时为必填项。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `nvo4z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| advertiser&#95;user&#95;id  <br />*可选*                            | 用于投放 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 广告的账号对应的 X 用户标识符。只有某些客户端应用程序可以使用此参数。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `312226591`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| audience&#95;expansion  <br />*可选*                                | 用于通过定向与已定向用户相似的用户来扩大广告系列的触达范围。<br /><br />**注意**：默认情况下，不会启用任何扩展。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `BROAD`, `DEFINED`, `EXPANDED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| bid&#95;strategy  <br />*可选*                                      | 竞价机制。<br /><br />`AUTO` 会根据每日预算和广告活动投放日期自动优化出价。<br /><br />`MAX` 用于设置允许的最高出价，当目标（objective）设置为 `REACH` 或 `FOLLOWERS` 时**不可用**。<br /><br />`TARGET` 会尝试使每日平均出价保持在指定的 `bid_amount_local_micro` 上下 20% 的范围内，当目标设置为 `REACH`、`FOLLOWERS` 或 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 时可用。<br /><br />**注意**：如果设置为 `AUTO`，将忽略 `bid_amount_local_micro`。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于目标。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `AUTO`, `MAX`, `TARGET`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| duration&#95;in&#95;days  <br />*可选项*                             | 在该时间周期内达到 `frequency_cap`。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />可能的取值：`1`, `7`, `30`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| entity&#95;status  <br />*可选*                                     | line item 的状态。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />默认值：`ACTIVE`  <br />可能的取值：`ACTIVE`、`DRAFT`、`PAUSED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| frequency&#95;cap  <br />*可选*                                     | 向单个用户展示该广告的最大次数。<br /><br />**注意**：仅在 `REACH`、`ENGAGEMENTS`、`VIDEO_VIEWS` 和 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 目标下受支持。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Example: `5`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| goal  <br />*可选*                                                  | 用于此 line item 的优化设置。<br /><br />`APP_PURCHASES` 选项适用于 `APP_INSTALL`。`APP_CLICKS` 和 `APP_INSTALLS` 选项同时适用于 `APP_INSTALL` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 目标，并且可能需要使用受支持的 [MACT 合作伙伴](https://business.x.com/en/help/campaign-setup/create-an-app-installs-or-app-engagement-campaign/mobile-app-conversion-tracking.html)。<br /><br />`SITE_VISITS` 选项仅适用于 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 目标。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于目标。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`APP_CLICKS`, `APP_INSTALLS`, `APP_PURCHASES`,`ENGAGEMENT`, `FOLLOWERS`, `LINK_CLICKS`, `MAX_REACH`, `PREROLL`, `PREROLL_STARTS`, `REACH_WITH_ENGAGEMENT`, `SITE_VISITS`, `VIDEO_VIEW`, `VIEW_3S_100PCT`, `VIEW_6S`, `VIEW_15S`, `WEBSITE_CONVERSIONS` |
| name  <br />*可选*                                                  | 此 line item 的名称。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />示例：`demo`<br /><br />最小和最大长度：`1`, `255`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| pay&#95;by  <br />*可选*                                            | 对该 line item 收费所依据的计费单位。此设置仅可在使用 `APP_INSTALLS` 目标的 line item 上进行修改。<br /><br />**注意**：默认的 `pay_by` 会根据 campaign 的目标和 line item 的出价单位自动设置。<br /><br />`APP_INSTALLS` 目标支持 `APP_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种值，其中 `IMPRESSION` 为默认值。<br /><br />`LINK_CLICKS` 目标支持 `LINK_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种值。`IMPRESSION` 为默认值，但在为 `bid_strategy` 设置 `TARGET` 时不受支持。<br /><br />`SITE_VISITS` 目标仅支持 `IMPRESSION` 值。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `APP_CLICK`, `IMPRESSION`, `LINK_CLICK`                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| standard&#95;delivery  <br />*可选*                                 | 启用标准投放或加速投放。请参阅 [Budget Pacing](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#budget-pacing) 以了解标准投放与加速投放的更多信息。仅当父级广告系列的 `budget_optimization` 设置为 `LINE_ITEM` 时可用<br /><br />类型：boolean<br /><br />默认值：`true`  <br />可能的取值：`true`、`false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| total&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选*       | 分配给该广告单元的总预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，$37.50 表示为 37500000。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `37500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| daily&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*在某些情况下必填* | 要分配给广告系列的每日预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，$5.50 表示为 5500000。若未提供，该广告系列会在其整个投放时长内，基于总预算均匀消耗预算。仅当父级广告系列的 `budget_optimization` 设置为 `LINE_ITEM` 时可用<br /><br />**注意**：此值应小于或等于 `total_budget_amount_local_micro`。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `5500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

**示例请求[](#example-request "指向此标题的永久链接")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items?campaign_id=hwtq0&objective=ENGAGEMENTS&product_type=PROMOTED_TWEETS&placements=ALL_ON_TWITTER&bid_amount_local_micro=3210000&entity_status=PAUSED&daily_budget_amount_local_micro=1000000&start_time=2022-06-15`

**示例响应[](#example-response "指向此标题的永久链接")**
