<div id="post-batchaccountsaccount_idline_items">
  #### POST batch/accounts/:account_id/line_items[](#post-batch-accounts-account-id-line-items "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

允许通过单个请求批量创建新的[广告项目（line items）](#post-accounts-account-id-line-items)。

**批量请求（Batch Requests）**

* 当前最大批量大小为 40。
* 所有参数都通过请求正文发送，并且必须使用 `Content-Type` 为 `application/json`。
* 批量请求作为一个整体，要么整体失败，要么整体成功；无论失败还是成功，所有 API 响应中的项目顺序都会与初始请求保持一致。

**批量响应（Batch Responses）**

批量 API 响应会返回按顺序排列的项目集合。除此之外，它们在结构上与对应的单个项目 endpoint 完全相同。

**批量错误（Batch Errors）**

* 请求级错误（例如超过最大批量大小）会在响应中的 `errors` 对象下返回。
* 项目级错误（例如缺少必需的广告项目参数）会在响应中的 `operation_errors` 对象下返回。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/:account_id/line_items`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                 | Description                                                                              |
| :----------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| operation&#95;type  <br />*required* | 正在对每个项目执行的操作类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可选值：`Create`、`Delete`、`Update`          |
| params  <br />*required*             | 一个 JSON 对象，包含广告项目对象的所有参数。有关必填和可选广告项目参数的列表，请参见[此处](#post-accounts-account-id-line-items)。 |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST 'Content-Type: application/json' https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items`

```json
    [
      {
        "operation_type":"Create",
        "params":{
          "campaign_id":"8yn7m",
          "objective":"ENGAGEMENTS",
          "product_type":"PROMOTED_TWEETS",
          "placements":"ALL_ON_TWITTER",
          "bid_amount_local_micro":3210000,
          "entity_status":"PAUSED"
        }
      }
    ]
```

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "name": null,
          "placements": [
            "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
          ],
          "start_time": null,
          "bid_amount_local_micro": 3210000,
          "advertiser_domain": null,
          "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
          "primary_web_event_tag": null,
          "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
          "end_time": null,
          "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
          "bid_strategy": "MAX",
          "duration_in_days": null,
          "standard_delivery": null,
          "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
          "id": "9cqi0",
          "entity_status": "PAUSED",
          "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
          "frequency_cap": null,
          "android_app_store_identifier": null,
          "categories": [],
          "currency": "USD",
          "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "created_at": "2017-07-07T17:42:20Z",
          "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
          "updated_at": "2017-07-07T17:42:20Z",
          "campaign_id": "8yn7m",
          "creative_source": "MANUAL",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ],
      "request": [
        {
          "params": {
            "placements": [
              "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
            ],
            "bid_amount_local_micro": 3210000,
            "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
            "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
            "entity_status": "PAUSED",
            "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
            "campaign_id": "8yn7m"
          },
          "operation_type": "Create"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="put-accountsaccount_idline_itemsline_item_id">
  #### PUT accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id[](#put-accounts-account-id-line-items-line-item-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

更新当前账户下指定的 line item。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                                                          | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| :---------------------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必填*                                  | 要操作的账户标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*必填*                            | 在请求中用于指定你要操作的 line item 的标识符。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| advertiser&#95;domain  <br />*可选*                           | 此广告主的网站域名，不含协议部分。<br /><br />**注意**：当 line item 的投放位置设置为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时必填。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `x.com`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| advertiser&#95;user&#95;id  <br />*可选*                      | 用于投放 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 广告的帐号所对应的 Twitter 用户标识符。只有某些客户端应用可以使用此参数。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `312226591`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| android&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*可选*        | 针对所推广应用的 Google 应用商店标识符。<br /><br />**注意**：`APP_INSTALLS` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 这两种目标要求至少设置一个应用商店标识符，即 `android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 中至少一个。<br /><br />类型：string<br /><br />示例：`com.twitter.android`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| audience&#95;expansion  <br />*可选*                          | 用于通过定向与已定向用户相似的用户来扩大广告系列的覆盖范围。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `BROAD`, `DEFINED`, `EXPANDED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| bid&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选*              | 要与此 line item 关联的出价金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，5.50 美元表示为 5,500,000（即 5500000）。<br /><br />**注意**：当 `bid_strategy` 设置为 `MAX` 或 `TARGET` 时为必填项。<br /><br />**注意**：只接受大于零的数值。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `140000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| bid&#95;strategy  <br />*可选*                                | 出价策略。<br /><br />`AUTO` 会根据每日预算和广告系列投放周期自动优化出价。<br /><br />`MAX` 设定允许的最高出价，当广告目标设置为 `REACH` 或 `FOLLOWERS` 时**不可用**。<br /><br />`TARGET` 尝试使每日平均出价控制在指定 `bid_amount_local_micro` 的 ±20% 范围内，并且在广告目标设置为 `REACH` 或 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 时可用。<br /><br />**注意**：如果设置为 `AUTO`，`bid_amount_local_micro` 将被忽略。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于广告目标。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `AUTO`, `MAX`, `TARGET`                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| categories  <br />*可选*                                      | 此广告主的相关 IAB 分类。请参阅 [GET iab&#95;categories](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#iab-categories)。<br /><br />**注意**：当 line item 的投放位置设置为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时，此字段为必填。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `IAB3-1`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| duration&#95;in&#95;days  <br />*可选*                        | 在该时间段内可达到 `frequency_cap` 设定的上限。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Possible values: `1`, `7`, `30`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| entity&#95;status  <br />*可选*                               | line item 的状态。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可能的值：`ACTIVE`、`PAUSED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| end&#95;time  <br />*可选*                                    | 以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 表示的该 line item 停止投放时间。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `2017-10-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| frequency&#95;cap  <br />*可选*                               | 可以向单个用户展示广告的最大次数。<br /><br />**注意**：仅适用于 `REACH`、`ENGAGEMENTS`、`VIDEO_VIEWS` 和 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 广告目标。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Example: `5`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| goal  <br />*可选*                                            | 用于此 line item 的优化设置。当目标为 `APP_INSTALL` 时，可使用 `APP_PURCHASES` 选项。`APP_CLICKS` 和 `APP_INSTALLS` 选项适用于 `APP_INSTALL` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS`，并且可能需要使用受支持的 [MACT 合作伙伴](https://business.x.com/en/help/campaign-setup/create-an-app-installs-or-app-engagement-campaign/mobile-app-conversion-tracking.html)。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于目标（objective）。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `APP_CLICKS`, `APP_INSTALLS`, `APP_PURCHASES`, `ENGAGEMENT`, `FOLLOWERS`, `LINK_CLICKS`, `MAX_REACH`, `PREROLL`, `PREROLL_STARTS`, `REACH_WITH_ENGAGEMENT`, `VIDEO_VIEW`, `VIEW_3S_100PCT`, `VIEW_6S`, `VIEW_15S`, `WEBSITE_CONVERSIONS` |
| ios&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*可选*            | 用于推广的应用在 Apple App Store 中的标识符的数字部分。<br /><br />**注意**：`APP_INSTALLS` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 投放目标要求至少设置一个应用商店标识符，即 `android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 至少设置其一。<br /><br />类型：string<br /><br />示例：`333903271`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| name  <br />*可选*                                            | 此投放项目的名称。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `demo`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| pay&#95;by  <br />*可选*                                      | 向该 line item 收费所依据的计费单位。仅对使用 `APP_INSTALLS` objective 的 line item 可以修改此设置。<br /><br />**注意**：默认的 `pay_by` 会根据 campaign objective 和 line item 的出价单位自动设置。<br /><br />`APP_INSTALLS` goal 同时支持 `APP_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种值，其中 `IMPRESSION` 为默认值。<br /><br />`LINK_CLICKS` goal 同时支持 `LINK_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种值，其中 `IMPRESSION` 为默认值，但在为 `bid_strategy` 设置 `TARGET` 时不支持该值。<br /><br />`SITE_VISITS` goal 支持 `IMPRESSION` 值。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `APP_CLICK`, `IMPRESSION`, `LINK_CLICK`                                                                                                             |
| start&#95;time  <br />*可选*                                  | 以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示的时间，即此 line item 的开始投放时间。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `2017-07-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| total&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 要分配给该 line item 的预算总金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，$37.50 表示为 37500000。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `37500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| daily&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 分配给广告系列的每日预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，$5.50 表示为 5500000。若未提供，将在整个广告系列投放周期内基于总预算均匀花费。仅当父级广告系列的 `budget_optimization` 设置为 `LINE_ITEM` 时可用<br /><br />**注意**：该值应小于或等于 `total_budget_amount_local_micro`。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `5500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`PUT https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items/9cqi0?bid_amount_local_micro=140000`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "9cqi0",
          "bid_amount_local_micro": 140000,
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "name": null,
        "placements": [
          "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
        ],
        "start_time": "2017-07-10T00:00:00Z",
        "bid_amount_local_micro": 140000,
        "advertiser_domain": null,
        "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
        "primary_web_event_tag": null,
        "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
        "end_time": null,
        "bid_strategy": "MAX",
        "duration_in_days": null,
        "standard_delivery": null,
        "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
        "id": "9cqi0",
        "entity_status": "PAUSED",
        "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
        "frequency_cap": null,
        "android_app_store_identifier": null,
        "categories": [],
        "currency": "USD",
        "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "created_at": "2017-07-07T17:42:20Z",
        "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
        "updated_at": "2022-06-03T23:51:36Z",
        "campaign_id": "8yn7m",
        "creative_source": "MANUAL",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idline_itemsline_item_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-line-items-line-item-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

删除当前账户下指定的 line item。

**注意**：删除 line item 后无法恢复，之后再次尝试删除该资源会返回 HTTP 404。

**注意**：当 line item 被删除后，只有在请求中指定 `with_deleted=true` 时，其子级 promoted&#95;tweets 才会在 GET accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted&#95;tweets 和 GET accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted&#95;tweets/:promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id 端点中返回。这些 promoted&#95;tweets 实际上并没有被删除（响应中 `"deleted": false`）。我们不会执行级联删除操作。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| :------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 投放账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中你要操作的 line item 的标识引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Exampple: `9f2ix`                                                                                                             |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items/9f2ix`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "bid_strategy": "MAX",
        "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "name": "Untitled",
        "placements": [],
        "start_time": null,
        "bid_amount_local_micro": 100000,
        "advertiser_domain": null,
        "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
        "primary_web_event_tag": null,
        "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
        "end_time": "2017-07-21T00:00:00Z",
        "duration_in_days": 1,
        "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
        "id": "9f2ix",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "frequency_cap": 5,
        "categories": [],
        "currency": "USD",
        "created_at": "2017-07-14T00:01:50Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-08-09T07:41:08Z",
        "campaign_id": "90r8n",
        "creative_source": "MANUAL",
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "9f2ix",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="line-item-curated-categories">
  ### 广告项目精选分类
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

有关使用的更多详细信息，请参阅[前贴片视频观看目标指南](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#video-views-preroll-objective)。

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idline_item_curated_categories">

</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部 line item curated category 的详细信息。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_item_curated_categories`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                       | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :----------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*           | 要操作账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 以外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与经过验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                 |
| count  <br />*optional*                    | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                 |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                   | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                           |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*              | 按受支持的属性以升序或降序排序。更多信息参见 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                        |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*         | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                     |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional* | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```josn
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "abc1"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "by5pw",
          "curated_category_id": "7op29tp2jzeo",
          "id": "1",
          "created_at": "2018-06-29T04:19:53Z",
          "updated_at": "2018-06-29T04:19:53Z",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/line_items/placements`

**Parameters[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                               | Description                                                                                                                    |
| :--------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| product&#95;type  <br />*optional* | 将响应范围限定为仅包含指定产品类型的有效投放位置。<br /><br />Type: enum（枚举）<br /><br />Possible values: `MEDIA`, `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT`, `PROMOTED_TWEETS` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
        "params": {
          "product_type": "PROMOTED_ACCOUNT"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idmedia_creatives">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/media_creatives[](#get-accounts-account-id-media-creatives "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账号关联的部分或全部媒体创意（media creatives）的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/media_creatives`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                         | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| :------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*             | 所使用账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 以外所有 Advertiser API 请求必需的参数。指定的账号必须与已通过身份验证的用户相关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                   |
| campaign&#95;id  <br />*optional*            | 将响应限定为仅包含与指定广告系列关联的媒体创意。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8gdx6`                                                                                                                                                         |
| count  <br />*optional*                      | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                         |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                     | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                             |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*optional*      | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含与指定推广单元（line items）关联的媒体创意。最多可以提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                             |
| media&#95;creative&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含所需的媒体创意。最多可以提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `1bzq3`                                                                                                                                |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*                | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                         |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*           | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                           |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional*   | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**Note**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**Note**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有较低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/media_creatives?media_creative_ids=1bzq3`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
          "media_creative_ids": [
            "1bzq3"
          ]
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "landing_url": "https://dev.x.com",
          "creative_type": "INTERSTITIAL_LANDSCAPE_TABLET",
          "id": "1bzq3",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-07-05T06:00:42Z",
          "account_media_id": "10miy",
          "updated_at": "2019-01-11T20:21:26Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idmedia_creatives">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/media_creatives[](#post-accounts-account-id-media-creatives "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将一个 [account media](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#account-media) 对象与指定的 line item 关联。

使用此端点在 Twitter Audience Platform 上投放视频贴片广告（当 account media 的 `creative_type` 为 `PREROLL` 时）或图片广告（例如 `BANNER` 或 `INTERSTITIAL`）。

**注意**：要将媒体资产添加到 Account Media 资源，请使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/media&#95;library](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#account-media) 端点。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/media_creatives`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                        | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| :------------------------------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />`required`            | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必填参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| account&#95;media&#95;id  <br />`required`  | 在此请求中要操作的 account media 实体的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `10miy`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />`required`      | 在此请求中要操作的 line item 的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| landing&#95;url  <br />`sometimes required` | 将用户引导至的网站的 URL。此字段仅应与 TAP 图片（或“展示型创意”）一起使用。如果用于 preroll 资产，此值会被忽略。要为 preroll 资产关联 URL，请使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/preroll&#95;call&#95;to&#95;actions](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#post-accounts-account-id-preroll-call-to-actions) 端点。<br /><br />**注意**：当 line item 的 objective 设置为 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 时为必填项。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `https://blog.x.com/` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/media_creatives?line_item_id=8v7jo&account_media_id=10miy`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "account_media_id": "10miy",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
        "landing_url": "https://dev.x.com",
        "creative_type": "INTERSTITIAL_LANDSCAPE_TABLET",
        "id": "1bzq3",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T06:00:42Z",
        "account_media_id": "10miy",
        "updated_at": "2019-01-11T20:21:26Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idmedia_creativesmedia_creative_id">

| media&#95;creative&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的媒体创意（media creative）的标识符。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `1bzq3`                                                                                                       |

**Example Request[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/media_creatives/1bzq3`

```json
    {
      "request": {

          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },

        "landing_url": "https://dev.x.com",

        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T06:00:42Z",
        "account_media_id": "10miy",
        "updated_at": "2021-04-16T21:02:55Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": true
      }
    }
```

### 推广账户

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accounts">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts "指向此标题的永久链接")
</div>

检索当前账号下一个或多个广告单元所关联的部分或全部推广账号的详细信息。

使用 [GET users/lookup](https://developer.x.com/en/docs/x-api/v1/accounts-and-users/follow-search-get-users/api-reference/get-users-lookup) 获取由响应中的 `user_id` 标识的用户账号的数据。

如果指定的广告单元中没有任何被配置为包含推广账号，则会返回 HTTP 400。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "指向此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts`

**Parameters[](#parameters "指向此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                           | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| :--------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*               | 广告主账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户相关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                        |
| count  <br />*optional*                        | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                         |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                       | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                                |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*optional*        | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含与这些广告单元关联的推广账号。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `9bpb2`                                                                                                                        |
| promoted&#95;account&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含所需的推广账号。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `19pl2`                                                                                                                               |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*                  | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                            |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*             | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                           |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional*     | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**Note**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**Note**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求会受到更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "指向此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts?promoted_account_ids=19pl2`

**Example Response[](#example-response "指向此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_account_ids": [
            "19pl2"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
          "user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "id": "19pl2",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accountspromoted_account_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts-promoted-account-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索当前账户下与某个 line item 关联的特定账户引用。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                          | Description                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| :-------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*              | 该账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promoted&#95;account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在本次请求中操作的推广账户的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `19pl2`                                                                                                                      |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*            | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                  |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts/19pl2`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_account_id": "19pl2",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
        "user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "id": "19pl2",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accounts">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts[](#post-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将一个账号（`user_id`）与指定的 line item 关联。

如果指定的 line item 未配置为可与 Promoted Accounts 关联，将返回 HTTP 400 `INCOMPATIBLE_LINE_ITEM` 错误。如果指定用户不符合推广资格，将返回 HTTP 400，且不会推广任何用户。如果提供的用户已经被推广，请求将被忽略。

有关 Promoted Accounts 的更多信息，请参阅我们的[广告系列管理](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#advertiser-api)页面。

**注意**：无法更新（PUT）Promoted Accounts 实体。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 该广告账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所需的必填参数。指定的账号必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                            |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的 line item 的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `9bpb2`                                                                                                                                                                    |
| user&#95;id  <br />*required*          | 在请求中要操作的用户的引用。使用 [GET users/lookup](https://developer.x.com/en/docs/x-api/v1/accounts-and-users/follow-search-get-users/api-reference/get-users-lookup) 根据 screen name 获取用户 ID。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `756201191646691328` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts?line_item_id=9bpb2&user_id=756201191646691328`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
        "user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "id": "19pl2",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accountspromoted_account_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts-promoted-account-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将某个账户与指定的 line item 解除关联。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                          | Description                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| :-------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*              | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promoted&#95;account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 用于标识与某个 line item 关联的 Promoted Account 实例的标识符。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `19pl2`                                                                                                |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts/19pl2`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
        "user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "id": "19pl2",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-08-23T18:53:15Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_account_id": "19pl2",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="promoted-tweets">
  ### 推广推文
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweets">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets "此标题的固定链接")
</div>

检索与当前账户下广告单元关联的推文引用。

使用 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/tweets](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#get-accounts-account-id-tweets) 端点来获取推文对象。对于每个 promoted&#95;tweets 对象，使用其 `tweet_id` 值。

**注意**：当父级广告单元被删除时，仅当在请求中指定 `with_deleted=true` 时才会返回这些 promoted&#95;tweets。这些 promoted&#95;tweets 实际上并未被删除（响应中 `"deleted": false`）。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的固定链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的固定链接")**

| Name                                         | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*             | 目标账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户相关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                   |
| count  <br />*optional*                      | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                   |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                     | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                           |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*optional*      | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含与特定广告单元关联的推文。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `96uzp`                                                                                                                    |
| promoted&#95;tweet&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含所需的推广推文。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `1efwlo`                                                                                                                        |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*                | 按支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息参见 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                        |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*           | 在请求结果中包含已删除的记录。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                   |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional*   | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有较低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的固定链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets?promoted_tweet_ids=1efwlo`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的固定链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_tweet_ids": [
            "1efwlo"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "96uzp",
          "id": "1efwlo",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-06-29T05:06:57Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-06-29T05:08:46Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "tweet_id": "880290790664060928",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweetspromoted_tweet_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets-promoted-tweet-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索当前账户下某个广告项目关联的特定推文引用。

**注意**：当父级广告项目被删除时，只有在请求中指定 `with_deleted=true` 时才会返回 `promoted_tweets`。不过这些 `promoted_tweets` 实际上并未被删除（响应中 `"deleted": false`）。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                        | Description                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :------------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*            | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（不包括 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts)）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的推广推文引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `1efwlo`                                                                                                                           |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*          | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                     |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets/1efwlo`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_tweet_id": "1efwlo",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "96uzp",
        "id": "1efwlo",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-06-29T05:06:57Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-06-29T05:08:46Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "tweet_id": "880290790664060928",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweets">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets[](#post-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将一个或多个推文关联到指定的订单行。并非所有推文都适合推广，这取决于广告活动目标。请参阅[基于目标的广告活动](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#objective-based-campaigns)以了解更多信息。

在使用 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 产品类型时，将某条推文与 `line_item` 关联，除了标准的 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 展示位置外，还会在移动端时间线中增加展示位置。

**注意**：无法更新（PUT）推广推文实体。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 用于投放账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在该请求中要操作的订单行的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                                          |
| tweet&#95;ids  <br />*required*        | 以逗号分隔的标识符列表，对应于特定推文。最多可以提供 50 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `822333526255120384`                                                                                             |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets?line_item_id=8v7jo&tweet_ids=822333526255120384`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "id": "1e8i2k",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-06-24T04:21:36Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-06-24T04:21:36Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "tweet_id": "822333526255120384",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ],
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "tweet_ids": [
            822333526255120384
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "total_count": 1
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweetspromoted_tweet_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets-promoted-tweet-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将指定广告单元中的一条推文解除关联。

**注意**：被删除的 promoted&#95;tweets 实体会在 ads.x.com 的 UI 中显示为“Paused”。同样地，在 UI 中执行“pausing”操作也会将该推文与其所属的广告单元解除关联。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                        | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| :------------------------------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*            | 用于投放的账户标识符。该参数出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 以外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                 |
| promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id  <br />*required* | 用于标识与某个广告单元关联的 Promoted Tweet 实例。该值来自 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted&#95;tweets](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets) 的响应条目中的 `id` 字段，而不是该推文本身的 `tweet_id`。在资源路径中提供。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `1gp8a5` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets/1gp8a5`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9pl99",
        "id": "1gp8a5",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-08-17T17:02:21Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-08-18T06:43:48Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "tweet_id": "844796297743757315",
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_tweet_id": "1gp8a5",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="promotable-users">
  ### 可推广的用户
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromotable_users">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promotable_users[](#get-accounts-account-id-promotable-users "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部可投放用户的详细信息。

可投放用户的类型为 `FULL` 或 `RETWEETS_ONLY`。这控制该账户被允许投放的内容类型。广告主必须获得推广其他用户内容的许可，并联系 Twitter，将这些用户以 `RETWEETS_ONLY` 类型的可投放用户形式添加到你的账户中。

在权限设置正确的前提下，你可以向推广产品相关的端点发起请求，直接引用你想要推广的推文的推文 ID。你可以使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted-tweets](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#promoted-tweets) 端点来推广已发布的推文，以及使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/scheduled-promoted-tweets](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#promoted-tweets) 端点来推广其他 Twitter Ads 账户的计划推文。

你不需要转推目标推文。以这种方式推广推文时，返回的 `tweet_id` 将与提供的推文 ID 不同。在后台，该推文会被作为一条 nullcasted 推文转推，然后再进行推广。返回的 `tweet_id` 对应的是这条新的推文。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promotable_users`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| count  <br />*optional* | 指定每个独立请求要尝试获取的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000` |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息请参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow` |
| promotable&#95;user&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为所需的可投放用户。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `l310s` |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional* | 按支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息请参见 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc` |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional* | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional* | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**Note**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**Note**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promotable_users?promotable_user_ids=l310s`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promotable_user_ids": [
            "l310s"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "id": "l310s",
          "created_at": "2016-07-21T22:42:09Z",
          "updated_at": "2016-07-21T22:42:09Z",
          "deleted": false,
          "promotable_user_type": "FULL"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromotable_userspromotable_user_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promotable_users/:promotable_user_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-promotable-users-promotable-user-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的指定可推广用户。

可推广用户的类型为 `FULL` 或 `RETWEETS_ONLY`。这将控制该账户被允许推广的内容类型。

广告主必须获得权限才能推广其他用户的内容。在正确设置权限后，你可以向推广产品相关的端点发起请求，直接引用你希望推广的推文的 Tweet ID。

你不必转推目标推文。使用这种方式推广推文时，返回的 `tweet_id` 将会不同于你提供的 Tweet ID。在后台，该推文会作为一条未对关注者展示的（nullcasted）推文被转推，然后再被推广。返回的 `tweet_id` 对应的是这条新的推文。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promotable_users/:promotable_user_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                         | Description                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| :------------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*             | 用于投放的账户标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promotable&#95;user&#95;id  <br />*optional* | 你在请求中要操作的可推广用户引用<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `l310s`                                                                                                                          |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*           | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                    |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promotable_users/l310s`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promotable_user_id": "l310s",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "user_id": "2417045708",
        "id": "l310s",
        "created_at": "2017-03-10T17:51:24Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-03-10T17:51:24Z",
        "deleted": false,
        "promotable_user_type": "RETWEETS_ONLY"
      }
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idrecommendations">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/recommendations[](#get-accounts-account-id-recommendations "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

状态：*封闭测试版*

检索与此广告账户关联的广告系列推荐。目前，每个资金工具仅限 1 条推荐。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/:account_id/recommendations`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                             | Description                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| :------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 所使用账户的标识符。该值出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户相关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/recommendations`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    "request": {
      "params": {
        "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
      }
    },
    "total_count": 1,
    "data": [
      {
        "funding_instrument_id": "gpvzb",
        "id": "62ce8zza1q0w",
        "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
        "status": "PENDING",
        "message": "测试推荐",
        "created_at": "2016-11-14T23:07:54Z",
        "updated_at": "2016-11-14T23:07:54Z"
      }
    ]
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idrecommendationsrecommendation_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/recommendations/:recommendation_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-recommendations-recommendation-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

状态：*封闭测试版（Closed Beta）*

检索与此广告账号关联的特定广告系列推荐。

广告系列推荐包含一整套针对广告系列结构的修改建议，并以对象树的形式表示。响应树旨在与 Batch API 端点配合使用，但也可以根据需要映射到单个更新端点（POST 表示 Create，PUT 表示 Update，DELETE 表示 Delete）。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/:account_id/recommendations/:recommendation_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 所使用账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| recommendation&#95;id  <br />*required* | 表示你在此次请求中要操作的推荐的 ID<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `62ce8zza1q0w` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/recommendations/62ce8zza1q0w`
