<div id="scheduled-promoted-tweets">
  ### 已排期的推广推文
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idscheduled_promoted_tweets">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/scheduled_promoted_tweets[](#get-accounts-account-id-scheduled-promoted-tweets "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部已排期推广推文的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/scheduled_promoted_tweets`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                                       | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :--------------------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                           | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                  |
| count  <br />*optional*                                    | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                   |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                                   | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                       |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*optional*                    | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含与特定广告单元相关的已排期推文。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8xdpe`                                                                                                                 |
| scheduled&#95;promoted&#95;tweet&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含所需的已排期推广推文。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `1xboq`                                                                                                                      |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*                              | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                   |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*                         | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                     |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional*                 | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有较低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/scheduled_promoted_tweets?scheduled_promoted_tweet_ids=1xboq`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "scheduled_promoted_tweet_ids": [
            "1xboq"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "8xdpe",
          "id": "1xboq",
          "created_at": "2017-06-01T19:53:32Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-06-01T20:00:06Z",
          "scheduled_tweet_id": "870366669373194240",
          "tweet_id": "870369382207070208",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idscheduled_promoted_tweetsscheduled_promoted_tweet_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/scheduled_promoted_tweets/:scheduled_promoted_tweet_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-scheduled-promoted-tweets-scheduled-promoted-tweet-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

获取与当前账户关联的特定预定推广推文。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/scheduled_promoted_tweets/:scheduled_promoted_tweet_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                                      | Description                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| :-------------------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                          | 投放账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| scheduled&#95;promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在此请求中要操作的预定推广推文的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `1xboq`                                                                                                                     |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*                        | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                   |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/scheduled_promoted_tweets/1xboq`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "scheduled_promoted_tweet_id": "1xboq",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "8xdpe",
        "id": "1xboq",
        "created_at": "2017-06-01T19:53:32Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-06-01T20:00:06Z",
        "scheduled_tweet_id": "870366669373194240",
        "tweet_id": "870369382207070208",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idscheduled_promoted_tweets">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/scheduled_promoted_tweets[](#post-accounts-account-id-scheduled-promoted-tweets "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将预定发布的推文关联到指定的 line item。

**注意**：无法对预定推广推文实体执行更新（PUT）操作。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/scheduled_promoted_tweets`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                         | Description                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :------------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*             | 所使用广告账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有广告主 API 请求所必需的参数（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 请求除外）。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 在请求中要操作的 line item 的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8xdpe`                                                                                                                    |
| scheduled&#95;tweet&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的预定推文的引用。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `870358555227860992`                                                                                                                |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/scheduled_promoted_tweets?line_item_id=8xdpe&scheduled_tweet_id=870358555227860992`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "8xdpe",
        "id": "1xtfl",
        "created_at": "2017-06-08T07:25:26Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-06-08T07:25:26Z",
        "scheduled_tweet_id": "870358555227860992",
        "tweet_id": null,
        "deleted": false
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "8xdpe",
          "scheduled_tweet_id": 870358555227860992,
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idscheduled_promoted_tweetsscheduled_promoted_tweet_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/scheduled_promoted_tweets/:scheduled_promoted_tweet_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-scheduled-promoted-tweets-scheduled-promoted-tweet-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将预定推文与指定的广告项目（line item）取消关联。

**注意**：只能在预定推文的 `scheduled_at` 时间*之前*删除 `scheduled_promoted_tweets`。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/scheduled_tweets/:scheduled_tweet_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                                      | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| :-------------------------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                          | 投放账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                     |
| scheduled&#95;promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id  <br />*required* | 指向你在请求中所操作的预定推广推文的引用。这是 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/scheduled&#95;promoted&#95;tweets](#get-accounts-account-id-scheduled-promoted-tweets) 响应对象中的 `id` 属性。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `1xtfl` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/scheduled_promoted_tweets/1xtfl`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "8xdpe",
        "id": "1xtfl",
        "created_at": "2017-06-08T07:25:26Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-06-15T05:14:12Z",
        "scheduled_tweet_id": "870358555227860992",
        "tweet_id": null,
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "scheduled_promoted_tweet_id": "1xtfl",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="targeting-criteria">
  ### 定向条件

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idtargeting_criteria">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/targeting_criteria[](#get-accounts-account-id-targeting-criteria "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户下推广线项关联的部分或全部定向条件的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/targeting_criteria`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                              | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| :------------------------------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                  | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                        |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*required*           | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含指定推广线项下的定向条件。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8u94t`                                                                                                                          |
| count  <br />*optional*                           | 指定每个单独请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                        |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                          | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                                 |
| lang  <br />*optional*                            | 一个 [ISO-639-1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_639-1) 语言代码。传入该参数时，如果对象存在本地化名称，响应中将返回一个额外的 `localized_name` 属性。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `fr`                                                                    |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*                     | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息参见 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                             |
| targeting&#95;criterion&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含所需的定向条件。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `dpl3a6`                                                                                                                              |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*                | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                           |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional*        | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**Note**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**Note**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/targeting_criteria?line_item_ids=8u94t`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
          "line_item_ids": [
            "8u94t"
          ]
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "8u94t",
          "name": "Custom audience targeting",
          "id": "dpl3a6",
          "operator_type": "EQ",
          "created_at": "2017-05-26T03:29:35Z",
          "targeting_value": "249yj",
          "updated_at": "2017-05-26T03:29:35Z",
          "deleted": false,
          "targeting_type": "CUSTOM_AUDIENCE"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idtargeting_criteriatargeting_criterion_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/targeting_criteria/:targeting_criterion_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-targeting-criteria-targeting-criterion-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的特定定向条件。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/targeting_criteria/:targeting_criterion_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                             | Description                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| :----------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                 | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| targeting&#95;criterion&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在该请求中要操作的定向条件的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `eijd4y`                                                                                                                       |
| lang  <br />*optional*                           | 一个 [ISO-639-1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_639-1) 语言代码。传入该参数时，如果对象存在本地化名称，响应中会额外返回一个 `localized_name` 属性。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `fr`                             |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*               | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                   |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/targeting_criteria/eijd4y`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "targeting_criterion_id": "eijd4y",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "619jl",
        "name": "🤖",
        "id": "eijd4y",
        "created_at": "2017-07-06T16:51:04Z",
        "targeting_value": "🤖",
        "updated_at": "2017-07-06T16:51:04Z",
        "deleted": false,
        "targeting_type": "BROAD_KEYWORD"
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idtargeting_criteria">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/targeting_criteria[](#post-accounts-account-id-targeting-criteria "跳转到此标题的永久链接")
</div>

请参阅 [Targeting Options](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#targeting-options) 页面，以查找特定定向类型对应的 `targeting_value`。我们建议每周刷新一次全部数据，以确保你使用的是最新的一组 targeting 类型取值。我们会不时更改可用的取值和定向条件；虽然大部分取值不会经常变动，但仍有一部分会发生变化，且无法保证这些取值保持不变。

对于 `BROAD_KEYWORD`、`EXACT_KEYWORD`、`PHRASE_KEYWORD` 或 `UNORDERED_KEYWORD` 定向类型，请使用在 `targeting_value` 中指定的关键词。若要排除关键词，请在请求参数中将 `operator_type` 设为 `NE`。有关每种类型的详细说明，请参阅 [targeting keyword types](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#targeting)。

**注意**：每个 line item 只能针对一个年龄分段进行投放。

**注意**：若要定向到某个 Custom Audience，该受众必须是可定向的，即 `targerable` *必须* 等于 `true`。

**注意**：当使用定向类型 `TV_SHOW` 时，在设置 `TV_SHOW` 定向之前，该 line item 上必须至少存在一个 `LOCATION` 定向条件，并且所有 `LOCATION` 都必须与所定向 `TV_SHOW` 的地域相同。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/targeting_criteria`

**Parameters[](#parameters "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

| Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 投放账户的标识符。该参数出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 以外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在本次请求中所操作的 line item 的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `69ob` |
| operator&#95;type  <br />*required* | 指定该定向条件应具有的关系。例如，要排除关键词，请使用 `operator_type=NE`。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `EQ`, `NE`, `GTE`, `LT` |
| targeting&#95;type  <br />*required* | 将应用于此 line item 的定向类型。<br /><br />可能的非关键词类取值包括：`AGE`、`DEVICE`、`EVENT`、`CAMPAIGN_ENGAGEMENT`、`CAMPAIGN_ENGAGEMENT_LOOKALIKE`、`CONVERSATION`、`ENGAGEMENT_TYPE`、`FOLLOWERS_OF_USER`、`GENDER`、`INTEREST`、`LANGUAGE`、`LIVE_TV_EVENT`、`LOCATION`、`NETWORK_ACTIVATION_DURATION`、`NETWORK_OPERATOR`、`PLATFORM`、`PLATFORM_VERSION`、`SIMILAR_TO_FOLLOWERS_OF_USER`、`TV_SHOW`、`USER_ENGAGEMENT`、`USER_ENGAGEMENT_LOOKALIKE`、`WIFI_ONLY`<br /><br />**注意**：每个 line item 只能针对一个 `AGE` 分段进行投放。<br /><br />可能的关键词类取值包括：`BROAD_KEYWORD`、`EXACT_KEYWORD`、`PHRASE_KEYWORD`、`UNORDERED_KEYWORD`<br /><br />可能的自定义受众类取值包括：`CUSTOM_AUDIENCE`、`CUSTOM_AUDIENCE_EXPANDED`<br /><br />可能的已安装 App 商店类别类取值包括：`APP_STORE_CATEGORY`、`APP_STORE_CATEGORY_LOOKALIKE`<br /><br />可能的 Twitter Audience Platform (TAP) App 排除类取值：`APP_LIST`（只能与 `operator_type=NE` 一起使用） |
| targeting&#95;value  <br />*required* | 根据所选的 `targeting_type`，指定此定向将应用到哪个用户、哪个兴趣、哪个地理位置、哪个事件、哪个平台、哪个平台版本、哪个设备、哪个关键词或短语、哪个性别、哪个自定义受众、哪个 App 商店类别，或要排除的哪个 App 列表。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `174958347` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/targeting_criteria?line_item_id=619jl&targeting_type=BROAD_KEYWORD&targeting_value=technology`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "619jl",
        "name": "technology",
        "id": "fbyjlr",
        "created_at": "2017-09-06T07:31:21Z",
        "targeting_value": "technology",
        "updated_at": "2017-09-06T07:31:21Z",
        "deleted": false,
        "targeting_type": "BROAD_KEYWORD"
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "619jl",
          "targeting_type": "BROAD_KEYWORD",
          "targeting_value": "technology",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-batchaccountsaccount_idtargeting_criteria">
  #### POST batch/accounts/:account_id/targeting_criteria[](#post-batch-accounts-account-id-targeting-criteria "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

允许通过单个请求批量创建新的定向条件（Targeting Criteria）。

**批量请求**

* 当前每个批次最多包含 500 个项目。
* 所有参数都在请求体中发送，且必须将 `Content-Type` 设置为 `application/json`。
* 批量请求要么作为整体失败，要么作为整体成功，对于错误和成功的所有 API 响应，都会保留与初始请求中项目相同的顺序。

**批量响应**

批量 API 响应返回一个有序的项目集合。除此之外，其结构与对应的单项目端点完全相同。

**批量错误**

* 请求级错误（例如，超出最大批量大小）会在响应中的 `errors` 对象下返回。
* 项目级错误（例如，缺少必需的定向条件参数）会在响应中的 `operation_errors` 对象下返回。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/:account_id/targeting_criteria`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                 | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| :----------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| operation&#95;type  <br />*required* | 对每个项目执行的操作类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`Create`、`Delete`                                                                                                                                                    |
| params  <br />*required*             | 包含所有定向条件对象参数的 JSON 对象。有关必需和可选定向条件参数列表，请参见[此处](#post-accounts-account-id-targeting-criteria)。<br /><br />此外，此端点支持一个 `operator_type` 参数，可与某些 `targeting_type` 值配合使用。此参数可能的取值为：`EQ` 表示等于，`GTE` 表示大于或等于，`LT` 表示小于，`NE` 表示不等于。 |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/targeting_criteria`

```json
    [
      {
        "operation_type":"Create",
        "params":{
          "line_item_id":"6f9an",
          "targeting_type":"LOCATION",
          "targeting_value":"5122804691e5fecc"
        }
      },
      {
        "operation_type":"Delete",
        "params":{
          "targeting_criterion_id":"al2rua"
        }
      }
    ]
```

**示例响应[](#example-response "指向本标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "data_type": "targeting_criterion",
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "6f9an",
          "name": "San Francisco-Oakland-San Jose CA, US",
          "id": "al7vt2",
          "location_type": "CITY",
          "operator_type": "EQ",
          "created_at": "2016-11-11T22:59:50Z",
          "targeting_value": "5122804691e5fecc",
          "updated_at": "2016-11-11T22:59:50Z",
          "deleted": false,
          "targeting_type": "LOCATION"
        },
        {
          "line_item_id": "6keuo",
          "name": "accounts",
          "id": "al2rua",
          "operator_type": "EQ",
          "created_at": "2016-11-11T17:50:19Z",
          "targeting_value": "accounts",
          "updated_at": "2016-11-11T22:59:50Z",
          "deleted": true,
          "targeting_type": "BROAD_KEYWORD"
        }
      ],
      "request": [
        {
          "params": {
            "line_item_id": "6f9an",
            "targeting_type": "LOCATION",
            "targeting_value": "5122804691e5fecc",
            "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
          },
          "operation_type": "Create"
        },
        {
          "params": {
            "targeting_criterion_id": "al2rua",
            "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
          },
          "operation_type": "Delete"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-targeting_criteriaapp_store_categories">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/app_store_categories[](#get-targeting-criteria-app-store-categories "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

获取可用于推广产品的基于应用商店分类的定向条件。应用商店分类目前仅适用于 iOS App Store 和 Google Play 商店。

已安装应用分类定向允许根据用户已安装或表示感兴趣的应用所属分类来进行定向。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/app_store_categories`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                          | Description                                                                             |
| :---------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| q  <br />*optional*           | 一个可选的查询参数，用于限定定向条件的范围。不传此参数则返回全部结果。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `music` |
| os&#95;type  <br />*optional* | 按特定应用商店限定返回结果。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `ANDROID`, `IOS`       |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/app_store_categories?q=music&os_type=IOS`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "name": "Games: Music",
          "targeting_type": "APP_STORE_CATEGORY",
          "targeting_value": "qouq",
          "os_type": "IOS"
        },
        {
          "name": "Music",
          "targeting_type": "APP_STORE_CATEGORY",
          "targeting_value": "qov2",
          "os_type": "IOS"
        }
      ],
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "q": "music",
          "os_type": "IOS"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="get-targeting_criteriaconversations">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/conversations[](#get-targeting-criteria-conversations "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

获取可用于推广产品的会话类定向条件。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/conversations`

**Parameters[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                    | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :-------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| conversation&#95;type  <br />*optional* | 一个可选查询，用于限定为某种特定的会话类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`ACTORS`, `ATHLETES`, `BOOK_GENRES`, `BOOKS`, `BRAND_CATEGORIES`, `BRANDS`, `CELEBRITIES`, `COACHES`, `DIGITAL_CREATORS`, `ENTERTAINMENT_BRANDS`, `ENTERTAINMENT_PERSONALITIES`, `FICTIONAL_CHARACTERS`, `JOURNALISTS`, `LIFESTYLES`, `MOVIE_GENRES`, `MOVIES`, `MUSIC_GENRES`, `MUSICIANS`, `NEWS_STORIES`, `NEWS`, `PERSONS`, `PLACES`, `PODCASTS`, `POLITICAL_AFFILIATIONS`, `POLITICIANS`, `PRODUCTS`, `RADIO_STATIONS`, `SPORTS_LEAGUES`, `SPORTS_PERSONALITIES`, `SPORTS_TEAMS`, `SPORTS`, `TRENDS`, `TV_SHOWS`, `VIDEO_GAME_PLATFORMS`, `VIDEO_GAME_PUBLISHERS`, `VIDEO_GAMES` |
| count  <br />*optional*                 | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />默认值：`200`  <br />最小值、最大值：`1`、`1000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [分页](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />示例：`8x7v00oow`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

**Example Request[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/conversations?count=2`

**Example Response[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "count": 2
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": "1f7m7",
      "data": [
        {
          "targeting_type": "CONVERSATION",
          "targeting_value": "a1",
          "name": "NFL",
          "conversation_type": "SPORTS"
        },
        {
          "targeting_type": "CONVERSATION",
          "targeting_value": "a2",
          "name": "NBA",
          "conversation_type": "SPORTS"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-targeting_criteriadevices">

| Name                    | Description                                                                                   |
| :---------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| count  <br />*optional* | 指定每个单独请求要检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000` |
| q  <br />*optional*     | 用于限定定向条件范围的可选查询参数。省略此参数以检索全部结果。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `apple`           |

<div id="get-targeting_criteriaevents">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/events[](#get-targeting-criteria-events "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

了解可用于推广产品（Promoted Products）的基于事件的定向条件。每个广告项目（line item）一次只能定向一个事件。

**注意**：同一事件往往会跨越多个时区，这在从跨时区视角考虑事件时间时会带来复杂性。为简化这一点，此端点上所有事件的 `start_time` 和 `end_time` 值一律统一表示为 UTC±00:00，与事件的本地语言环境和时区无关。在查询和使用事件的 `start_time` 与 `end_time` 值时，应牢记这一设计。例如，美国的独立日会在 UTC±00:00 中表示为 `start_time=2017-07-04T00:00:00Z` 和 `end_time=2017-07-05T00:00:00Z`，从而避免该节日在美国境内横跨多个时区所带来的问题。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/events`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                | Description                                                                                                                                                    |
| :---------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| event&#95;types  <br />*required*   | 查询参数，用于限定到特定的事件类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`CONFERENCE`、`HOLIDAY`、`MUSIC_AND_ENTERTAINMENT`、`OTHER`、`POLITICS`、`RECURRING`、`SPORTS`             |
| count  <br />*optional*             | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />默认值：`200`  <br />最小值、最大值：`1`、`1000`                                                                         |
| country&#95;codes  <br />*optional* | 可选查询参数，用 2 字母 ISO 国家代码将定向条件搜索限定到特定国家。如果未指定此参数，则返回所有事件。<br /><br />Type: string                                                                                 |
| cursor  <br />*optional*            | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />示例：`8x7v00oow`                                                 |
| end&#95;time  <br />*optional*      | 以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示的广告活动结束时间。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />示例：`2017-10-05T00:00:00Z`                               |
| start&#95;time  <br />*optional*    | 以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示的广告项目开始投放时间。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值为当前时间。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />示例：`2017-07-05T00:00:00Z` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/events?count=1`

**响应示例[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "count": 1
        }
      },
      "data_type": "events",
      "data": [
        {
          "reach": {
            "total_reach": null
          },
          "name": "New Year's",
          "start_time": "2017-12-31T00:00:00Z",
          "top_users": [],
          "top_tweets": [],
          "top_hashtags": [],
          "gender_breakdown_percentage": {},
          "end_time": "2018-01-02T00:00:00Z",
          "country_code": null,
          "device_breakdown_percentage": {},
          "targeting_value": "1ex",
          "is_global": true,
          "event_type": "HOLIDAY",
          "country_breakdown_percentage": {}
        }
      ],
      "next_cursor": "uww0"
    }
```

<div id="get-targeting_criteriainterests">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/interests[](#get-targeting-criteria-interests "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

发现可用于推广产品（Promoted Products）的基于兴趣的定向条件。兴趣集合变更不频繁，但我们建议你至少每周刷新一次该列表。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/interests`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                     | Description                                                                                                              |
| :----------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| count  <br />*optional*  | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                             |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow` |
| q  <br />*optional*      | 可选查询字符串，用于限定定向条件范围。省略此参数将检索全部结果。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `books`                                     |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/interests?q=books`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "name": "Books and literature/Biographies and memoirs",
          "targeting_type": "INTEREST",
          "targeting_value": "1001"
        }
      ],
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "q": "books",
          "count": 1
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": "6by4n4"
    }
```

<div id="get-targeting_criterialanguages">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/languages[](#get-targeting-criteria-languages "固定链接到此标题")
</div>

查询可用于广告定向的语言。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "固定链接到此标题")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/languages`

**Parameters[](#parameters "固定链接到此标题")**

| Name                     | Description                                                                                                              |
| :----------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| count  <br />*optional*  | 指定每个独立请求中要尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                         |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow` |
| q  <br />*optional*      | 可选查询，用于限定某个定向条件。省略此参数将检索全部结果。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `english`                                      |

**Example Request[](#example-request "固定链接到此标题")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/languages?q=english`

**Example Response[](#example-response "固定链接到此标题")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "name": "English",
          "targeting_type": "LANGUAGE",
          "targeting_value": "en"
        }
      ],
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "q": "english"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null
    }
```

<div id="get-targeting_criterialocations">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/locations[](#get-targeting-criteria-locations "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

发现可用于推广产品的基于位置的定向条件。地理定向适用于推广账号和推广推文，可在国家级、州/地区级、城市级以及邮政编码级别进行。若希望在邮政编码级别获取分析数据，则必须使用邮政编码定向。

**注意**：要检索特定可定向城市（例如 San Francisco 或 New York），请在请求参数 `location_type` 中使用枚举值 `CITIES`。

要定向指定市场区域（DMA，Designated Market Areas），请使用枚举值 `METROS`。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/locations`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| count  <br />*optional* | 指定每次独立请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000` |
| country&#95;code  <br />*optional* | 可选查询参数，通过 2 位 ISO 国家代码将定向条件搜索限定在特定国家。省略此参数以检索所有国家的结果。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `JP` |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定一个游标以获取下一页结果。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow` |
| location&#95;type  <br />*optional* | 按特定类型的位置限定结果。比 `COUNTRIES` 更细粒度的定向在某些地区可能不可用。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `COUNTRIES`, `REGIONS`, `METROS`, `CITIES`, `POSTAL_CODES` |
| q  <br />*optional* | 可选查询参数，用于限定定向条件搜索范围。省略此参数以检索所有结果。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `New York` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/locations?location_type=CITIES&q=los angeles`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json

      "data": [
        {

          "targeting_type": "LOCATION"
        },
        {
```

<div id="get-targeting_criterianetwork_operators">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/network_operators[](#get-targeting-criteria-network-operators "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

查找可用于推广产品的基于网络运营商的定向条件。

此端点使你能够在多个国家/地区查询可用于定向的运营商，例如 AT&amp;T、Verizon、Sprint、T-Mobile 等。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/network_operators`

**Parameters[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                               | Description                                                                                                       |
| :--------------------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| count  <br />*optional*            | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />默认值: `200`  <br />最小值、最大值: `1`, `1000`                             |
| country&#95;code  <br />*optional* | 可选查询，用于将定向条件搜索限定到具有 2 个字母 ISO 国家代码的特定国家/地区。如果未指定此参数，则只会返回美国的合作伙伴受众。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />默认值: `US`    |
| cursor  <br />*optional*           | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `8x7v00oow` |
| q  <br />*optional*                | 可选查询，用于限定定向条件搜索范围。省略此参数以检索所有结果。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `Airpeak`                                    |

**Example Request[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/network_operators?count=5&country_code=US`

**Example Response[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "country_code": "US",
          "targeting_type": "NETWORK_OPERATOR",
          "name": "Advantage",
          "targeting_value": "2l"
        },
        {
          "country_code": "US",
          "targeting_type": "NETWORK_OPERATOR",
          "name": "Aeris",
          "targeting_value": "1b"
        },
        {
          "country_code": "US",
          "targeting_type": "NETWORK_OPERATOR",
          "name": "Airadigm",
          "targeting_value": "2t"
        },
        {
          "country_code": "US",
          "targeting_type": "NETWORK_OPERATOR",
          "name": "Airlink PCS",
          "targeting_value": "14"
        },
        {
          "country_code": "US",
          "targeting_type": "NETWORK_OPERATOR",
          "name": "Airpeak",
          "targeting_value": "1i"
        }
      ],
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "country_code": "US",
          "count": 5
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": "o7x9iet1a5u608olj4"
    }
```

<div id="get-targeting_criteriaplatform_versions">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/platform_versions[](#get-targeting-criteria-platform-versions "跳转到此标题的永久链接")
</div>

查询适用于推广产品的、基于移动操作系统版本的可用定向条件。平台版本定向可用于推广帐号和推广推文。这使你能够将定向精确到移动操作系统版本的小版本号，例如 Android 8.0 或 iOS 10.0。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/platform_versions`

**参数[](#parameters "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                | Description                                                                                  |
| :------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| q  <br />*optional* | 可选查询字符串，用于限定定向条件搜索范围。省略此参数以检索所有结果。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Examples: `jelly bean` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/platform_versions`

**响应示例[](#example-response "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
        "data": [
            {...},
            {
                "name": "Ice Cream Sandwich",
                "number": "4.0",
                "os_type": "Android",
                "targeting_type": "PLATFORM_VERSION",
                "targeting_value": "17"
            },
            {
                "name": "Jelly Bean",
                "number": "4.1",
                "os_type": "Android",
                "targeting_type": "PLATFORM_VERSION",
                "targeting_value": "18"
            },
            {...}
        ],
        "data_type": "targeting_criterion",
        "request": {
            "params": {}
        }
    }
```

<div id="get-targeting_criteriaplatforms">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/platforms[](#get-targeting-criteria-platforms "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

发现可用于推广产品（Promoted Products）的基于平台的定向条件。

| q  <br />*optional*     | 一个可选查询，用于限定定向条件搜索范围。省略此参数以检索全部结果。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Examples: `ios`, `blackberry`                                                          |
| lang  <br />*optional*  | 使用 [ISO-639-1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_639-1) 语言代码。传入该参数时，响应中会额外返回一个 localized&#95;name 属性。<br /><br />Type: int, string<br /><br />Example: `fr` |

<div id="get-targeting_criteriatv_shows">
  #### GET targeting_criteria/tv_shows[](#get-targeting-criteria-tv-shows "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

查找可用于推广产品的电视节目定向条件。电视节目定向目前仅在特定市场的推广推文中提供。可用市场请参阅 [GET targeting&#95;criteria/tv&#95;markets](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-targeting-criteria-tv-markets) 端点。

**注意**：任何包含少于 1,000 名用户的受众，其 `estimated_users` 值都会显示为 `1000`。

**注意**：不再支持电视频道和节目类型（genre）定向选项。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/tv_shows`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                     | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :----------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| locale  <br />*required* | 必填参数，用于指定要查询可用电视节目的 tv&#95;market&#95;locale。电视市场是根据 [GET targeting&#95;criteria/tv&#95;markets](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts-account-id-targeting-criteria) 返回的 `locale` 进行查询的。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `en-US` |
| count  <br />*optional*  | 指定每次独立请求中尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `50`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `50`                                                                                                                                                        |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                                                |
| q  <br />*optional*      | 可选查询参数，用于限定定向条件搜索范围。如果省略此参数，将检索所有结果。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Examples: `ios`, `blackberry`                                                                                                                                               |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/targeting_criteria/tv_shows?locale=en-US&q=news&count=1`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```jdon
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "name": "NewsWatch",
          "targeting_value": 10027243420,
          "genre": "PAID",
          "locales": [
            {
              "language": "en",
              "country": "US"
            }
          ]
        }
      ],
      "next_cursor": "c-22838-zdQDJrTxSvOYfQOhb2IlGQ",
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "locale": {
            "countryCode": "US",
            "languageCode": "en"
          },
          "count": 1,
          "q": "news"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="targeting-suggestions">
  ### 定向建议
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idtargeting_suggestions">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/targeting_suggestions[](#get-accounts-account-id-targeting-suggestions "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

获取最多 50 条关键词或用户定向建议，用于补充您最初的选择。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/targeting_suggestions`

**Parameters[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| :------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 要使用的账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#accounts) 除外）。指定的账户必须与进行身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| suggestion&#95;type  <br />*required*  | 指定要返回的建议类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `KEYWORD`, `USER_ID`                                                                                                            |
| targeting&#95;values  <br />*required* | 以逗号分隔的一组关键词或用户 ID，作为生成建议的种子。<br /><br />**Note**: 这两种类型的建议不能混用。<br /><br />Example: `756201191646691328`                                                                                      |
| count  <br />*optional*                | 指定每个单独请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `30`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `50`                                                                                                    |

**Example Request[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/targeting_suggestions?suggestion_type=KEYWORD&targeting_values=developers&count=2"`

**Example Response[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "suggestion_type": "KEYWORD",
          "suggestion_value": "devs"
        },
        {
          "suggestion_type": "KEYWORD",
          "suggestion_value": "software"
        }
      ],
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "suggestion_type": "KEYWORD",
          "targeting_values": [
            "developers"
          ],
          "count": 2,
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="tax-settings">

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idtax_settings">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/tax_settings[](#get-accounts-account-id-tax-settings "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

获取与当前账户关联的税务设置详情。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/tax_settings`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                             | Description                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所需的必填参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/tax_settings`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {

        }
      },
      "data": {
        "tax_id": "GB896391250",

        "address_postal_code": "SW1A 1AA",
        "bill_to": "NOT_SET",
        "address_region": "London",
        "address_country": "GB",
        "address_first_name": null,
        "invoice_jurisdiction": "NOT_SET",
        "address_street2": null,
        "address_email": null
      }
    }
```

<div id="put-accountsaccount_idtax_settings">
  #### PUT accounts/:account_id/tax_settings[](#put-accounts-account-id-tax-settings "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

更新当前账户的税务设置。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/tax_settings`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| 名称                                                 | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| :------------------------------------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*                         | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与当前已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| address&#95;city  <br />*可选*                       | 账户所有者地址所在城市。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `San Francisco`                                                                                                                        |
| address&#95;country  <br />*可选*                    | 账户所有者地址对应的两位字母国家代码。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `US`                                                                                                                            |
| address&#95;email  <br />*可选*                      | 与账户所有者地址关联的电子邮箱地址。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `api@mctestface.com`                                                                                                             |
| address&#95;first&#95;name  <br />*可选*             | 账户所有者地址中的名。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />示例: `API`                                                                                                                                        |
| address&#95;last&#95;name  <br />*可选*              | 账户所有者地址中填写的姓氏。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `McTestface`                                                                                                                         |
| address&#95;name  <br />*可选*                       | 账户所有者地址的公司名称。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `ABC, Co.`                                                                                                                            |
| address&#95;postal&#95;code  <br />*可选*            | 账户持有人地址的邮政编码。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `94102`                                                                                                                               |
| address&#95;region  <br />*可选*                     | 账户所有者地址所在地区。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `California`                                                                                                                           |
| address&#95;street1  <br />*可选*                    | 账户所有者地址中的第一行街道信息。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `21 March St`                                                                                                                     |
| address&#95;street2  <br />*可选*                    | 账户所有者地址的第二行街道。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `Suite 99`                                                                                                                           |
| bill&#95;to  <br />*可选*                            | 计费对象。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`ADVERTISER`, `AGENCY`                                                                                                                             |
| business&#95;relationship  <br />*可选*              | 该账户是由广告主持有还是由代理商持有。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`AGENCY`、`SELF`                                                                                                                      |
| client&#95;address&#95;city  <br />*可选*            | 广告主地址所在的城市。<br /><br />当广告账户由代理机构拥有时需设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `Toronto`                                                                                               |
| client&#95;address&#95;country  <br />*可选项*        | 广告主地址的两个字母的国家代码。<br /><br />当广告账户由代理机构持有时，请设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `CA`                                                                                              |
| client&#95;address&#95;email  <br />*可选*           | 与广告主地址关联的电子邮件地址。<br /><br />当广告账户由代理机构拥有时设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `ads@brand.com`                                                                                     |
| client&#95;address&#95;first&#95;name  <br />*可选*  | 广告主地址中的名字（名）。<br /><br />当广告账户由代理机构拥有时设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `Brand`                                                                                                |
| client&#95;address&#95;last&#95;name  <br />*可选*   | 广告主地址中的姓氏。<br /><br />当广告账户由代理机构所有时设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `Advertiser`                                                                                              |
| client&#95;address&#95;name  <br />*可选项*           | 广告主地址中的公司名称。<br /><br />当广告账户由代理机构持有时设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `Brand, Inc.`                                                                                           |
| client&#95;address&#95;postal&#95;code  <br />*可选* | 广告主地址的邮政编码。<br /><br />当广告账户归代理机构所有时，请设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `M5H 2N2`                                                                                              |
| client&#95;address&#95;region  <br />*可选*          | 广告主地址所在的省/州/地区。<br /><br />当该广告账户由代理机构持有时需设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `Ontario`                                                                                          |
| client&#95;address&#95;street1  <br />*可选*         | 广告主地址的街道地址第一行。<br /><br />当广告账户归代理机构所有时设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />示例: `280 Queen St W`                                                                                           |
| client&#95;address&#95;street2  <br />*可选*         | 广告主地址的第二行街道地址。<br /><br />当广告账户由代理机构拥有时，请设置此字段。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `The 6`                                                                                             |
| invoice&#95;jurisdiction  <br />*可选*               | 发票适用的司法管辖区。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `LOI_SAPIN`, `NONE`, `NOT_SET`                                                                                                    |
| tax&#95;category  <br />*可选*                       | 税务应按个人主体还是企业主体征收。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `BUSINESS_NO_VAT`, `BUSINESS_WITH_VAT`, `INDIVIDUAL`                                                                        |
| tax&#95;exemption&#95;id  <br />*可选*               | 增值税免税编号。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `12345`                                                                                                                                    |
| tax&#95;id  <br />*可选*                             | 增值税登记 ID。<br /><br />类型：string<br /><br />可能的取值：`67890`                                                                                                                                         |

**示例请求[](#example-request "指向此标题的永久链接")**

`PUT https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/tax_settings?address_name=ABC, Co.`

**示例响应[](#example-response "指向此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
          "address_name": "ABC Co."
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "tax_id": "GB896391250",
        "address_city": "London",
        "business_relationship": "SELF",
        "address_street1": "21 March St",
        "address_last_name": null,
        "address_company": "ABC, Co.",
        "tax_category": "BUSINESS_WITH_VAT",
        "address_postal_code": "SW1A 1AA",
        "bill_to": "NOT_SET",
        "address_region": "London",
        "address_country": "GB",
        "address_first_name": null,
        "invoice_jurisdiction": "NOT_SET",
        "address_street2": null,
        "address_email": null
      }
    }
```

<div id="tracking-tags">
  ### 跟踪标签
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idtracking_tags">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/tracking_tags[](#get-accounts-account-id-tracking-tags "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部追踪标签的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/tracking_tags`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                       | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| :----------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*           | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                        |
| count  <br />*optional*                    | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                                         |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                   | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                             |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*optional*    | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为与特定投放单元关联的追踪标签。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `96uzp`                                                                                                                             |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*              | 按受支持的属性以升序或降序排序。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                          |
| tracking&#95;tag&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，仅返回所需的追踪标签。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `3m82`                                                                                                                                        |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*         | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                           |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional* | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**Note**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**Note**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/tracking_tags?tracking_tag_ids=3m82`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "tracking_tag_ids": [
            "3m82"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "fdwcl",
          "tracking_tag_url": "https://ad.doubleclick.net/ddm/trackimp/N1234.2061500TWITTER-OFFICIAL/B9156151.125630439;dc_trk_aid=1355;dc_trk_cid=8675309",
          "tracking_tag_type": "IMPRESSION_TAG",
          "id": "3m82",
          "created_at": "2019-06-26T17:04:26Z",
          "updated_at": "2019-06-26T17:04:26Z",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idtracking_tagstracking_tag_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/tracking_tags/:tracking_tag_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-tracking-tags-tracking-tag-id "固定链接到此标题")
</div>

获取与当前账户关联的特定跟踪标签。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "固定链接到此标题")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/tracking_tags/:tracking_tag_id`

**参数[](#parameters "固定链接到此标题")**

| Name                                      | Description                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| :---------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*          | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| tracking&#95;tag&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的跟踪标签的标识。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `555j`                                                                                                                           |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*        | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                    |

**示例请求[](#example-request "固定链接到此标题")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/tracking_tags/555j`

**示例响应[](#example-response "固定链接到此标题")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "with_deleted": true,
          "tracking_tag_id": "555j",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "72v2x",
        "tracking_tag_url": "https://ad.doubleclick.net/ddm/trackimp/N6344.2061500TWITTER-OFFICIAL/B23028778.279118262;dc_trk_aid=473354132;dc_trk_cid=119658253",
        "tracking_tag_type": "IMPRESSION_TAG",
        "id": "555j",
        "created_at": "2020-08-13T23:02:03Z",
        "updated_at": "2020-08-13T23:02:03Z",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idtracking_tags">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/tracking_tags[](#post-accounts-account-id-tracking-tags "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

将跟踪标签与指定的订单项关联。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/tracking_tags`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                        | Description                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :------------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*            | 所使用的账户标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（不包括 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts)）所必需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required*      | 你在请求中操作的订单项的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                                            |
| tracking&#95;tag&#95;type  <br />*required* | 跟踪标签的类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `IMPRESSION_TAG`, `CLICK_TRACKER`                                                                                                   |
| tracking&#95;tag&#95;url  <br />*required*  | 跟踪合作伙伴提供的跟踪标签 URL。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `https://ad.doubleclick.net/ddm/trackimp/N1234.2061500TWITTER-OFFICIAL/B9156151.125630439;dc_trk_aid=1355;dc_trk_cid=8675309`   |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "fdwcl",
          "tracking_tag_type": "IMPRESSION_TAG",
          "tracking_tag_url": "https://ad.doubleclick.net/ddm/trackimp/N1234.2061500TWITTER-OFFICIAL/B9156151.125630439;dc_trk_aid=1355;dc_trk_cid=8675309",

      },

        "tracking_tag_url": "https://ad.doubleclick.net/ddm/trackimp/N1234.2061500TWITTER-OFFICIAL/B9156151.125630439;dc_trk_aid=1355;dc_trk_cid=8675309",
```

<div id="put-accountsaccount_idtracking_tagstracking_tag_id">

| tracking&#95;tag&#95;url  <br />*required* | 跟踪合作伙伴提供的跟踪标签 URL。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `https://ad.doubleclick.net/ddm/trackimp/N1234.2061500TWITTER-OFFICIAL/B9156151.125630439;dc_trk_aid=1355;dc_trk_cid=8675309`       |

```json
        }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idtracking_tagstracking_tag_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/tracking_tags/:tracking_tag_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-tracking-tags-tracking-tag-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将指定的追踪标签与指定的广告单元解除关联。

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/tracking_tags/:tracking_tag_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                      | Description                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| :---------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*          | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源的路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所需的参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| tracking&#95;tag&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的追踪标签的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `555j`                                                                                                                           |

<div id="user-settings">
  ### 用户设置
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

(https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87)

<div id="get-accountsaccount_iduser_settingsuser_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/user_settings/:user_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-user-settings-user-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索用户设置。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/user_settings/:user_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                             | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| :------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求所必需的参数。  <br />指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。  <br />  <br />Type: string  <br />  <br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| user&#95;id  <br />*required*    | 你在请求中要操作的用户引用。使用 GET users/lookup 接口根据某个 screen name 检索对应的 user ID。  <br />  <br />Type: long  <br />  <br />Example: `756201191646691328`                                                                    |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/user_settings/756201191646691328`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
      {
        "request": {
          "params": {
            "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
            "user_id": "756201191646691328"
          }
        },
        "data": {
          "notification_email": "user@domain.com",
          "contact_phone": "",
          "contact_phone_extension": ""
        }
      }
```

<div id="put-accountsaccount_iduser_settingsuser_id">
  #### PUT accounts/:account_id/user_settings/:user_id[](#put-accounts-account-id-user-settings-user-id "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

更新用户设置。需要用户上下文。账号管理员无法调用。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/user_settings/:user_id`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                              | Description                                                                                                                                                                 |
| :------------------------------------------------ | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                  | 所使用账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中以及 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts)。  <br />指定的账号必须与已认证用户关联。  <br />  <br />Type: string  <br />  <br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| user&#95;id  <br />*required*                     | 在请求中你所操作的用户引用。使用 GET users/lookup 通过 screen name 获取用户 ID。  <br />  <br />Type: long  <br />  <br />Example: `756201191646691328`                                            |
| notification&#95;email  <br />*optional*          | 用于账号通知的电子邮件地址。  <br />  <br />Type: string  <br />  <br />Example: `user@domain.com`                                                                                        |
| contact&#95;phone  <br />*optional*               | 联系电话号码。  <br />  <br />Type: string  <br />  <br />Example: `202-555-0128`                                                                                                  |
| contact&#95;phone&#95;extension  <br />*optional* | 联系电话 `contact_phone` 的分机号。  <br />  <br />Type: string  <br />  <br />Example: `1234`                                                                                       |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`PUT https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/user_settings/756201191646691328?notification_email='user@domain.com'&subscribe_email_types=ACCOUNT_PERFORMANCE,PERFORMANCE_IMPROVEMENT"`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
      {
        "request": {
          "params": {
            "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
            "user_id": "756201191646691328"
            "notification_email": "user@domain.com",
            "subscribed_campaign_events": [
              "ACCOUNT_PERFORMANCE",
              "PERFORMANCE_IMPROVEMENT"
            ]
          }
        },
        "data": {
          "notification_email": "user@domain.com",
          "contact_phone": "",
          "Contact_phone_extension": ""
        }
      }
```
